### [23] [SimpleWikiSearch: A Clean Offline Wikipedia Environment for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26070)
*Guanming Xiong,Penghui Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SimpleWikiSearch 是一个明确指定和可复现的基于维基百科的智能搜索评估环境，包含完整的语料构建、检索栈、工具接口和评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于 LLM 的智能搜索系统往往只关注底层 LLM 的性能，而忽略了搜索环境（如维基百科快照、预处理流程、分块策略、检索后端、工具模式、观察格式和答案提交规则）对结果的影响。这些细节常常未充分说明，导致结果难以比较或复现。

Method: 从完整的英文维基百科 dump 出发，经过清洗和分块构建语料库，构建关键词和稠密检索索引，暴露由 search、open_url 和 submit_answer 组成的简化工具接口。提供了领域特定的智能体工具框架和受控离线环境，并在六个 QA 数据集上使用开源 LLM 报告基线结果，同时提供 random-300 子集用于与闭源商业模型比较。

Result: 在六个 QA 数据集上使用开源 LLM 报告了基线结果，并提供了用于与闭源商业模型比较的 random-300 子集。

Conclusion: SimpleWikiSearch 提供了一个可复现的智能搜索评估参考环境，其贡献在于指定了完整的参考设置而非新的智能体算法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agentic search systems are often evaluated as if the underlying LLM were the only component that matters, yet their measured performance also depends on the surrounding search environment: the Wikipedia snapshot, preprocessing pipeline, chunking policy, retrieval backend, tool schema, observation format, and answer submission rule. These details are frequently under-specified, making it difficult to compare results or reproduce reported baselines. We present SimpleWikiSearch, whose corpus construction, retrieval stack, tool contract, and evaluation protocol are explicit and runnable. The environment starts from a full English Wikipedia dump, cleans and chunks the corpus, builds keyword and dense retrieval indexes, and exposes a minimal tool interface consisting of \texttt{search}, \texttt{open\_url}, and \texttt{submit\_answer}. We report baseline results on six QA datasets using open-source LLMs and provide a random-300 subset for comparisons with closed-source commercial models. SimpleWikiSearch provides a domain-specific agent harness and a controlled offline environment for reproducible agentic-search evaluation. Its contribution is this specified reference setup, rather than a new agent algorithm. Code and data will be available at: https://github.com/JimXiongGM/simple_wiki_search.

</details>


### [24] [GuidedRAG: Semantic Steering of Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26071)
*Matthijs Jansen op de Haar,Tobias Stähle,Lorenzo Gatti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GuidedRAG通过在检索前引入语义导向的选择阶段，将知识库与用户意图对齐，显著提升检索相关性（14-15.8%）、缓解精度损失（19.7-27.4%），并将检索开销降低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法依赖日益复杂的检索和知识结构，但检索空间与用户意图的对齐不足，导致检索精度损失和开销过大。

Method: 在传统RAG中引入专用的选择阶段和语义导向（semantic steering），在检索前利用语义约束知识库，将检索空间与用户意图对齐并大幅缩小搜索空间。

Result: 检索相关性提升14.0-15.8%，检索精度损失缓解19.7-27.4%，检索开销降低数个数量级；相关块在排序中更靠前；用户意图对齐度提升31.8-36.8%；在15种不同RAG变体上实现全覆盖。

Conclusion: 语义导向和选择是一种有效且可泛化的范式，能够改进当前RAG的最新技术水平。

Abstract: In this work, we propose GuidedRAG, a novel extension to traditional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) that introduces a dedicated selection stage and semantic steering during retrieval. In contrast to current state-of-the-art RAG approaches, which depend on increasingly complex retrieval and knowledge structures, GuidedRAG constrains the knowledge base using semantics before retrieval, aligning the retrieval space with user intent while substantially reducing the search space. Our evaluation shows that GuidedRAG improves retrieval relevance by 14.0-15.8%, mitigates a 19.7-27.4% loss in retrieval precision, and reduces retrieval overhead by orders of magnitude. Moreover, relevant chunks are consistently retrieved earlier in the ranking process, while alignment with user intent improves by 31.8-36.8%. We further show that GuidedRAG achieves full coverage across 15 diverse RAG variants, demonstrating generalizability across the literature. Together, these findings establish semantic steering and selections as a powerful and generalizable paradigm for improving the current state-of-the-art in RAG.

</details>


### [25] [IFCMemoryBench: Evaluating Long-Term Memory of LLM-Based Agents in BIM Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26072)
*Changyu Du,Alexander Vosseler,Filippo Mazza,André Borrmann*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出IFCMemoryBench基准，用于评估LLM代理在建筑信息建模（BIM）领域的长时记忆能力。该基准包含143个多会话任务、19个项目、4016个先前会话。测试发现最强记忆系统在真实部署场景下仅达32.4%的准确率，揭示当前通用记忆系统在专业领域存在领域迁移鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的长时记忆评估主要关注开放域或人格化的对话回忆测试，缺乏在结构化、领域特定的动态环境中评估代理能否跨会话复用信息的能力。作者选择BIM这一专业工程工作流作为测试场景，因其要求代理同时查询大型IFC模型并依赖对话中讨论但模型未包含的项目规范、客户决策和工程惯例。

Method: 基于IFC-Bench v2中信息不完整的问题构建IFCMemoryBench，包含143个多会话任务、19个项目、4016个先前会话。每个任务通过早期对话埋入缺失的项目上下文，后续提出只能通过结合记忆上下文和实时IFC查询来回答的探测问题。评估框架将记忆性能分解为摄入、检索和利用三个维度，使用专家验证的LLM裁判评估答案质量和记忆质量。

Result: 评估了向量型、图型和文件型三种代表性记忆系统。最强系统在真实部署摄入范围下仅达32.4%的答案准确率，即使在Oracle过滤摄入或更强探测代理条件下仍低于60%。分析表明当前通用记忆系统虽能检索主题相关上下文，但存储的项目知识不完整或碎片化。

Conclusion: 当前通用记忆系统在专业领域存在显著的领域迁移鸿沟，可靠的专业代理需要领域感知的记忆表示，能够连接对话、项目知识和结构化模型实体。

Abstract: Long-term memory is becoming a core capability of LLM-based agents, but existing evaluations largely test conversational recall in open-domain or persona-grounded settings. We argue that a stronger test is whether an agent can reuse information from prior sessions while acting over a live, structured, domain-specific environment. We study this problem in Building Information Modelling (BIM), a professional engineering workflow where agents must query large IFC models while also relying on project specifications, client decisions, and engineering conventions often discussed in conversation but absent from the model. We introduce IFCMemoryBench, a benchmark for evaluating long-term memory in LLM-based BIM information retrieval. IFCMemoryBench contains 143 multi-session tasks across 19 projects and 4,016 prior sessions, derived from incomplete-information questions in IFC-Bench v2. Each task seeds missing project context across earlier conversations and later asks a probe question that can be answered only by combining remembered context with live IFC queries. Our evaluation framework decomposes memory performance into ingestion, retrieval, and utilization, and measures both answer quality and memory quality with expert-validated LLM judges. We evaluate representative vector-, graph-, and file-based memory systems. The strongest system achieves only 32.4% answer accuracy under a deployment-realistic ingestion scope, and remains below 60% under oracle-filtered ingestion or a stronger probe agent. Analysis shows that current general-purpose memory systems often retrieve topically relevant context but store project knowledge as incomplete or fragmented facts. These results reveal a domain-transfer gap in agent memory and suggest that reliable professional agents require domain-aware memory representations linking conversations, project knowledge, and structured model entities.

</details>


### [26] [Guess Where You Go: Generative Next Point-of-Interest Recommendation in Amap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26073)
*Penglong Zhai,Bowen Zheng,Jie Li,Yifang Yuan,Yue Liu,Sicong Wang,Mingyang Yin,Tingting Hu,Shuaijun Guo,Fanyi Di,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Gwhere是一个端到端工业级框架，通过对比残差量化分词器生成判别性语义标识（SID），并结合LLM进行生成式下一兴趣点（POI）推荐，在公共数据集和高德大规模工业数据集上有效提升了推荐性能，已部署于高德首页服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索在工业场景中面临两大挑战：一是token分配存在冗余或冲突，二是异构物品信号（文本、视觉、空间、协同）整合不足。这些问题在下一POI推荐任务中尤为突出，因为模型需要表示结构化空间实体、捕捉序列移动模式并生成符合真实用户行为的预测。

Method: 提出Gwhere框架，包含：(1) 对比残差量化（contrastive residual-quantization）分词器，对齐文本、视觉、空间和协同信号，学习判别性POI语义标识(SID)；(2) 基于SID适配LLM到移动场景，通过丰富时空语料上的继续预训练、有监督微调以及Exposure-Aware Kahneman-Tversky Optimization (EAKTO) 强化学习目标进行行为偏好对齐。

Result: 在公共数据集和高德大规模工业数据集上验证有效性；长期在线A/B测试显示，相比生产基线，P-CTR提升5.83%，U-CTR提升6.20%。

Conclusion: Gwhere通过创新性的SID生成与LLM结合，有效解决了工业级生成式POI推荐中的token冗余/冲突和信号整合问题，并在高德首页高并发低延迟的真实场景中成功部署，证明了其工业实用性。

Abstract: Generative retrieval enables recommender systems to retrieve items by generating compact item identifiers, but scaling it to industrial scenarios remains challenging due to redundant or colliding token assignments and insufficient integration of heterogeneous item signals. These challenges are particularly critical for next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation, where models must represent structured spatial entities, capture sequential mobility patterns, and produce predictions consistent with real user behavior. We propose Gwhere, an end-to-end industrial framework that integrates semantic identifier (SID) generation with LLM-based generative next POI recommendation. Gwhere first learns discriminative POI SIDs through a contrastive residual-quantization tokenizer that aligns textual, visual, spatial, and collaborative signals. Based on these SIDs, Gwhere adapts LLMs to mobility scenarios via continued pretraining on enriched spatio-temporal corpora, supervised fine-tuning, and Exposure-Aware Kahneman-Tversky Optimization (EAKTO), a reinforcement learning objective for behavioral preference alignment. Experiments on public datasets and Amap's large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of Gwhere. The system has been deployed in Amap's homepage service under high-concurrency and low-latency constraints. Long-term online A/B tests show improvements of 5.83% in P-CTR and 6.20% in U-CTR over the production baseline. The implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/alibaba/SimCIT.

</details>


### [27] [Reproducibility in Recommender Systems: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26074)
*Alan Said,Alejandro Bellogin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了ACM RecSys会议可重复性赛道（2020-2025年）的51篇论文，发现该赛道从单纯的复现/复制扩展至基准测试、资源和方法论贡献；可重复性论文在数据集、算法和评估协议上趋于一致；实践中常以扩展实验替代严格复现。尽管提升了透明度和文档质量，但对方法论多样性的影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重复性已成为推荐系统研究可信度的基石，但学界对实验结果可靠性和泛化性的担忧日益增长。ACM RecSys于2020年设立专门的可重复性赛道以促进严谨、透明和可重复的研究，本文旨在系统回顾该赛道的发展历程，理解可重复性在社区中的实际运作方式。

Method: 对2020-2025年RecSys可重复性赛道收录的51篇论文进行结构化分析，按贡献类型分类，并分析数据集、算法、框架和评估实践中的常见模式。

Result: 三大发现：(1) 赛道范围从复现/复制扩展到基准测试、资源和方法论贡献；(2) 可重复性论文呈现一致的方法论特征，依赖有限的数据集、算法和评估协议；(3) 实践中可重复性工作更倾向于扩展先前的实验（引入额外模型或评估标准），而非严格复现。

Conclusion: 可重复性工作提升了透明度和文档质量，但对方法论多样性的影响有限，揭示了可重复性的概念定义与其实际实施之间存在差距。

Abstract: Reproducibility has become a cornerstone of credible recommender systems research, driven by growing concerns about the reliability and generalizability of experimental results. In response, the ACM RecSys conference introduced a dedicated Reproducibility Track in 2020 to encourage rigorous, transparent, and repeatable research. This paper presents a structured analysis of the track from 2020 to 2025, covering 51 accepted papers. We classify contributions by type and analyze common patterns in datasets, algorithms, frameworks, and evaluation practices, with the goal of understanding how reproducibility is operationalized in practice within the community.
  Our findings reveal three main trends. First, the track has expanded in scope, evolving from a focus on reproduction and replication to include benchmarking, resources, and methodological contributions. Second, reproducibility papers exhibit a consistent methodological profile, relying on a limited set of datasets, algorithms, and evaluation protocols. Third, reproducibility in practice often involves extending prior experiments rather than strictly replicating them, with studies frequently introducing additional models or evaluation criteria. Overall, reproducibility work has improved transparency and documentation, but has had limited impact on methodological diversity, highlighting a gap between the conceptual definition of reproducibility and its implementation.

</details>


### [28] [IDP AutoOpt: Agent-Driven Optimization of Document Processing Pipeline Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26075)
*David Kaleko,Sergey Ivanov,Md Mofijul Islam*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出IDP AutoOpt，一个自主LLM智能体，能够自动调优智能文档处理管线的配置（提示词、模型、OCR设置、架构等），在多个企业场景中匹配或超越人类专家准确率，并将配置时间从数周缩短至两小时以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前IDP管线的调优依赖领域专家手动完成，每类文档需要20到80+人小时，无法随企业文档种类的增加而扩展，成为部署瓶颈。

Method: 闭环优化框架：在小标注集上评分配置→诊断字段级错误→生成针对性修改→重新评估。结合人类编写的领域技能编码生产经验，并允许配置可编程的管线、评分函数和小标注集。

Result: 在抽取基准上准确率达90.2%（vs人类专家81.6%），每页成本降低4.6倍；配置时间从数周降至2小时内；发现LLM智能体能力存在硬阈值、结构化领域技能优于原始代码访问。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅适用于IDP，还可推广到其他企业AI系统（如RAG、多智能体工作流）的配置自动化，仅需可配置管线、评分函数和小标注集。

Abstract: We present IDP AutoOpt, an autonomous LLM agent that discovers high-performing configurations for intelligent document processing (IDP) pipelines. Tuning IDP prompts, models, OCR settings, and schemas jointly currently costs domain specialists 20 to 80+ person-hours per document type and does not scale as enterprises add document classes. IDP AutoOpt runs a closed loop: it scores a configuration on a small labeled set, diagnoses field-level errors, generates targeted edits, and re-evaluates, guided by human-authored domain skills that encode production expertise. Across extraction, classification, and packet-splitting tasks deployed in healthcare, marketing-intelligence, and financial-services settings, IDP AutoOpt matches or exceeds human-expert accuracy at equal or lower cost (on an extraction benchmark, 90.2% vs 81.6% at 4.6 x lower per-page cost), cutting configuration time from weeks to under two hours. We further show that agent LLM capability has a hard threshold below which optimization fails, and that curated domain skills outperform raw source-code access, which can degrade performance when provided without structure. We also share practical lessons on context management and variance mitigation. Requiring only a configurable pipeline, a scoring function, and a small labeled set, the approach extends beyond IDP to other enterprise AI systems, such as RAG and multi-agent workflows, where configuration bottlenecks deployment.

</details>


### [29] [FinCacheServe: Dependency-Consistent Answer Reuse for Cost-Efficient RAG Serving over Mutable Enterprise Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26076)
*Lingteng Zeng,Yifan Jin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FinCacheServe是一个面向可变企业文档的检索增强生成服务缓存系统，通过依赖一致性检查实现了53%以上的LLM调用跳过率，且无依赖过时的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可变企业文档上重复执行语义等价的分析请求时，GPU-bound生成工作可以复用，但现有响应缓存难以保证依赖一致性——当文档、证据块、工具输出发生变化时，缓存可能返回过时结果。

Method: 将每个生成的答案视为服务对象，按企业意图索引，并由文档版本、证据指纹、工具指纹、模型身份和解码配置共同守卫。基于vLLM实现，使用Qwen2.5模型评估SEC财务文档工作负载。

Result: 在2,230请求的7B模型trace上跳过53.27%的LLM调用且零依赖过时输出；在三个32B模型种子集上跳过53.31%的544个请求，优于版本化语义缓存（38.97%）和基于grounded的重用（22.43%）；每依赖新鲜2秒SLO成功预估能耗比版本化语义缓存低44.30%。

Conclusion: FinCacheServe通过细粒度的依赖一致性守卫机制，在保证输出新鲜度的前提下显著提升了缓存命中率，为可变文档场景下的RAG服务提供了高效的答案复用方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation services over mutable enterprise documents repeatedly execute semantically equivalent analysis requests. Answer reuse can remove GPU-bound generation work, yet response caches require dependency consistency when filings, evidence chunks, and tool outputs change. FinCacheServe treats each generated answer as a serving object indexed by enterprise intent and guarded by document versions, evidence fingerprints, tool fingerprints, model identity, and decoding configuration. A vLLM implementation evaluates SEC-derived financial-document workloads with Qwen2.5 models. On a 2,230-request hosted 7B trace, FinCacheServe skips 53.27% of LLM calls with zero observed dependency-stale outputs. Across three hosted 32B operator-suite seeds, it skips 53.31% of 544 requests, compared with 38.97% for versioned semantic caching and 22.43% for grounded-style reuse. Capacity, backend, and SLO replays show oracle-bounded cache management, 100k-entry transactional metadata behavior, and 44.30% lower estimated Wh per dependency-fresh 2s-SLO success than versioned semantic caching.

</details>


### [30] [Embedding Items at Scale: Comparing GNN-Based and ID-Based Item Embeddings in the Yandex Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26365)
*Sergei Makeev,Artem Matveev,Vladimir Baikalov,Kirill Khrylchenko*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过Yandec的三项生产系统（Market、Music、Lavka）对比了预训练GNN物品嵌入与端到端可训练物品嵌入在Transformer序列推荐中的效果，发现预训练在数据有限时有益，但在大规模数据集上无显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer序列推荐模型依赖物品嵌入策略，但缺乏在工业大规模场景下从成本和质量角度对比预训练嵌入与端到端学习嵌入的工作。

Method: 在Yandex Market和Yandex Music两个成熟生产推荐系统以及Yandex Lavka低资源数据集上，对比预训练工业GNN物品嵌入与端到端可训练物品嵌入的效果。

Result: 预训练阶段在训练数据有限时有助于提升效果，但在大规模数据集上训练时，预训练嵌入并未带来有价值的收益。

Conclusion: 对于大规模工业推荐系统，端到端学习物品嵌入足以取得良好效果，预训练仅在低资源场景下有价值。

Abstract: Transformer-based sequential recommendation models, which process sequences of user-item interactions, rely heavily on the item embedding strategy. Existing approaches either use pretrained item embeddings or learn them end-to-end with the transformer. To the best of our knowledge, no prior work has compared these options from both cost and quality perspectives in a large-scale industrial setting. This paper is a case study that compares pretrained industrial graph neural network item embeddings with end-to-end trainable item embeddings across two mature production recommendation systems at Yandex: Yandex Market and Yandex Music. We additionally evaluate both approaches on a low-resource dataset sampled from Yandex Lavka production logs, for which both the data and code are publicly available for demonstration purposes. Our results show that a separate pretraining stage helps when training data is limited, but provides no worthwhile benefit for large-scale models trained on extensive datasets.

</details>


### [31] [Continuous Online Evaluation of Recommendation Strategies in Social Science Academic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26380)
*Mehmet Deniz Türkmen,Daniel Hienert*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过在GESIS Search（社会科学领域学术搜索引擎）中集成和评估多种推荐系统，发现基于语义相似性的推荐方法优于传统的词项相似性和基于会话的推荐方法，但不同信息类型下推荐效果存在差异，并展示了如何利用STELLA框架进行持续实时评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术搜索引擎中由于学科领域、信息类型和用户偏好的多样性，提供相关推荐是一项复杂任务，需要解决如何有效整合和评估多种推荐策略的问题。

Method: 采用STELLA评估框架在GESIS Search中持续实时评估多种推荐策略，包括传统词项相似性、基于Transformer嵌入的语义文档相似性以及基于历史用户会话点击路径的会话推荐。

Result: 基于语义相似性的推荐获得了用户最高偏好，优于词项相似性和会话方法；但不同信息类型（如研究数据、出版物、变量、测量工具）下推荐效果存在差异。

Conclusion: 持续评估机制可帮助开发更符合学术搜索门户用户偏好的推荐系统，且信息寻求行为因信息类型而异，需要差异化推荐策略。

Abstract: Delivering relevant recommendations in academic search engines is a complex task due to the diversity of subject areas, information types, and user preferences. In this case study, we address these challenges by integrating and evaluating a range of recommendation systems within GESIS Search - a domain-specific search engine for the social sciences that provides researchers with access to research data, publications, variables, and measurement instruments. To support continuous, real-time evaluation of multiple recommendation strategies with actual platform users, we utilize the STELLA evaluation framework. We implement and compare a diverse set of algorithms, including traditional lexical similarity, semantic document similarity by using transformer-based embeddings, and session-based recommendations based on click paths from historical user sessions. Our results show that users prefer recommendations based on semantic similarity, which outperformed term-similarity and session-based methods. However, the performance of recommenders varies across categories within GESIS Search, suggesting that information-seeking behavior differs by information type. Overall, our study provides insights into how continuous evaluation can be incorporated to develop recommendations that better align with the preferences in academic search portals.

</details>


### [32] [DIRECTOR: Dynamic Index-based Recommendation with Transport-Optimized Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26418)
*Yuanhao Pu,Chenghao Zhang,Chao Feng,Xiang Li,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DIRECTOR是一种基于最优传输的并行重排序框架，通过连续潜空间映射和动态检索索引生成，在保持并行效率的同时实现全局结构协调，在工业推荐场景中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式重排序方法中，自回归模型虽能捕捉位置间依赖关系，但在贪婪或有限宽度解码下会过早剪枝全局最优排列且存在顺序延迟；非自回归模型通过位置并行预测提升效率，但位置独立建模导致跨位置协调不足，易产生重复或冲突选择。因此需要一种既能保持并行效率又能引入全局结构协调的重排序方法。

Method: DIRECTOR将候选物品映射到连续潜空间，为所有目标位置并行生成查询条件动态检索索引；训练时使用熵正则化最优传输（OT）提供冲突感知监督；推理时直接在相似矩阵上进行全局硬匹配以产生无重复的候选列表；引入前缀锚定信用分配机制，将全局奖励转化为位置级训练信号以对齐生成器与黑盒列表评估器。

Result: 大量离线和在线实验表明，DIRECTOR在大型工业推荐场景中持续优于强基线重排序方法，取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: DIRECTOR通过最优传输引导的并行重排序框架，有效解决了自回归模型的顺序延迟和非自回归模型的位置独立性问题，为工业级推荐系统提供了一种高效且全局协调的重排序方案。

Abstract: Reranking is a combinatorial decision problem that aims to select and order a high-utility slate from a request-specific candidate set. A major line of generative rerankers adopts autoregressive (AR) models, which construct the slate one position at a time to capture inter-position dependencies. However, under practical greedy or bounded-width decoding, prefix-based search may prematurely prune globally promising permutations and incurs inherently sequential latency, restricting the effective search space under a fixed serving budget. Non-autoregressive (NAR) alternatives alleviate this efficiency bottleneck through position-parallel prediction, but naive position-wise factorization treats different positions too independently, leading to insufficient cross-position coordination and potentially duplicate or conflicting item selections. To retain parallel efficiency while introducing global structural coordination, we propose Dynamic Index-based RECommendation with Transport-Optimized Retrieval (DIRECTOR), a transport-guided parallel reranking framework. DIRECTOR maps candidate items into a continuous latent space and generates request-conditioned dynamic retrieval indices for all target positions in parallel. During training, it uses entropy-regularized OT to provide conflict-aware supervision; at inference, it directly performs global hard matching on similarity matrix, producing duplicate-free slates without iterative transport. To further align the generator with an opaque list-wise evaluator that returns only a scalar utility, we introduce a prefix-anchored credit assignment mechanism that converts the global reward into position-specific training signals. Extensive offline and online experiments demonstrate that DIRECTOR consistently outperforms strong reranking baselines, achieving significant improvement in large-scale industrial recommendation scenarios.

</details>


### [33] [PSG: Pair-Space Generation for Efficient Generative Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26427)
*Chao Feng,Li Ma,Xiancheng Gao,Chenghao Zhang,Yuanhao Pu,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Pair-Space Generation (PSG)，将生成式推荐中的生成原子从单个物品提升为有序物品对，通过二次词汇表实现约2倍加速，并在快手平台部署后实现用户停留时间提升0.178%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐系统采用自回归模型作为骨干网络，存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 复杂度随列表长度线性增长，在严格延迟约束下只能生成较少候选序列，限制了探索空间；(2) Teacher-forcing训练方式导致训练-测试不匹配，累积误差随序列长度增加而劣化生成质量。

Method: 提出Pair-Space Generation (PSG)，将生成原子从单个物品重新定义为有序物品对，词汇表大小扩展为n(n-1)，但生成步数减少至L/2。采用预训练的pair-token表示模块在大规模曝光日志上优化，解决二次词汇表带来的数据稀疏问题。从理论上证明了PSG与物品空间生成的双射关系、约2-4倍的理论加速比，以及在结果奖励下子最优性界为O((L/2)²\barε)。

Result: 理论分析表明PSG在中等设置下可实现约2-4倍加速，实际工业环境中达到1.83倍加速；子最优性界相比物品空间生成提升近4倍。在快手平台（超4亿日活用户）部署后，用户停留时间提升0.178%。

Conclusion: PSG通过将生成空间从物品提升到物品对，在不损失表达能力的前提下，有效缓解了自回归生成式推荐的效率瓶颈和累积误差问题，理论分析与工业部署均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems adopt Generator-Evaluator (G-E) for list-wise reranking: a generator produces sequences from candidates and an evaluator scores them at sequence-level to filter out the optimal one for exposure. Auto-Regressive(AR), working as the backbone for generative recommendation, suffers two limitations. First, its complexity grows linearly with list length, forcing the system to generate fewer lists under rigorous latency constraints and thus limiting exploration. Second, teacher-forcing creates a train-test mismatch; cumulative errors worsen with length and degrade quality.
  To address these problems, we propose Pair-Space Generation (PSG), a reformulation that elevates the generation atom from individual items to ordered item pairs. Given $n$ candidate items, PSG operates over pair vocabulary of size $n(n-1)$ per request, generates only $L/2$ tokens. Pair token representations are produced on-the-fly by a pretrained pair-token representation module optimized over large scale exposure logs, eliminating the data sparsity that would otherwise plague a quadratic sized vocabulary. We establish three theoretical guarantees: (i) PSG is bijective with item-space generation and induces an equivalent family of sequence distributions, thus incurring no loss of expressiveness; (ii) generation in pair-token space achieves approximately a $2\times$ to $4\times$ speedup theoretically under moderate settings and $1.83\times$ in the real industrial environmental settings; and (iii) under outcome-only rewards, the worst-case suboptimality of PSG is bounded by $O((L/2)^2 \barε)$, representing a nearly $4\times$ improvement over item-space generation. Beyond benchmark-based validation, PSG has also been deployed on Kuaishou, delivering a 0.178\% lift in per-user stay time on the platform, which serves over 400 million daily active users.

</details>


### [34] [NMKFR: A Robust Framework for Time-Aware Cold-Start Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26429)
*Chengzhi Liu,Ning Zeng,Zehui Qu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出神经记忆卡尔曼融合推荐框架（NMKFR），通过Titans语义编码器与时序卡尔曼状态追踪相结合，利用后验协方差作为不确定性信号校准语义-时序融合，在物品冷启动推荐任务上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物品冷启动推荐面临新物品早期交互稀疏且推荐环境随时间变化的挑战，静态内容、早期反馈和时序状态证据各有价值但可靠性在物品生命周期中变化不一。

Method: 提出NMKFR框架，包含两个分支：1）基于Titans的语义编码器从文本中提取增强记忆的物品观测；2）时域卡尔曼状态追踪在不规则交互间隔下估计潜在状态。利用后验协方差作为不确定性信号来校准语义记忆检索和自适应静态-时序融合。

Result: 在Amazon Video Games和MovieLens-32M数据集上，采用时间感知和物品冷启动协议进行采样候选排序评估，NMKFR在所有对比、消融、诊断和鲁棒性分析中取得最强保留结果，并表现出有界的不确定性相关内部行为。

Conclusion: 实验证据表明后验协方差指导的语义-时序融合在离线评估设置下是有效的。

Abstract: Item cold-start recommendation is difficult when new items have sparse early interactions and appear in recommendation environments that keep changing over time. Static content, early feedback, and temporal-state evidence are all useful, but their reliability varies across the item lifecycle. This work proposes a framework--Neural Memory Kalman Fusion Recommender (NMKFR), which combines a Titans-based semantic encoder with time-aware Kalman state tracking. The semantic branch extracts memory-enhanced item observations from text, while the temporal branch estimates latent states under irregular interaction intervals. The NMKFR further uses posterior covariance as an uncertainty signal to calibrate semantic memory retrieval and adaptive static-temporal fusion. Experiments on Amazon Video Games and MovieLens-32M evaluate NMKFR under time-aware and item cold-start protocols using sampled candidate ranking. Across the reported comparisons, ablations, diagnostics, and robustness analyses, NMKFR achieves the strongest retained results and exhibits bounded uncertainty-related internal behavior. These findings provide empirical evidence for posterior-covariance-guided semantic-temporal fusion under the evaluated offline settings.

</details>


### [35] [Multi-Decoder OneRec: Controllable Generative Retrieval for Multi-Objective Industrial Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26500)
*You Wang,Zhao Liu,Guoping Tang,Yiqing Yang,Shuo Su,Jing Liu,Naifu Zhou,Xiaoyou Zhou,Wei Jiang,Jian Liang,Xiao Lv,Ruiming Tang,Liyin Hong,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Multi-Decoder OneRec框架，通过共享表示、独立目标适配和协调解码，在生成式检索中实现多目标控制，在公开数据集和工业A/B测试中均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统中，基于显式配额的检索路线设计导致建模、训练和服务碎片化；而基于语义ID的单解码器生成式检索将目标策略纠缠在一起，限制了候选互补性。

Method: 共享用户上下文模块和通用解码器；每个目标添加独立的LoRA专家；训练时采用梯度路由隔离更新（曝光样本NTP更新共享基座、目标过滤NTP更新事件专家、KL正则策略优化更新观看时长专家）；推理时通过显式路线配额分配固定预算，并使用多解码器约束束搜索减少跨路线重叠。

Result: 在512检索预算下，Recall@512提升1.69%-5.62%；A/B测试中app使用时长提升0.37%，Day-7留存提升0.19%，分享设备提升0.19%，冷启内容提升2.09%。同时发布Kwai26大规模多目标基准数据集。

Conclusion: 生成式检索能够将共享建模与目标特定控制、互补候选生成相结合，为工业推荐系统提供统一且可控的解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems build candidate pools by assigning explicit quotas to objective-specific retrieval routes. This design offers quota control but increasingly fragments modeling, training, and serving as the route set grows. Semantic-ID-based generative retrieval provides a unified alternative, yet a single decoder entangles objective policies and limits candidate complementarity. We propose Multi-Decoder OneRec, a controllable framework that combines shared representations, isolated objective adaptation, and coordinated decoding. All objectives share a user-context module and the General Decoder, while each objective adds an isolated, parameter-efficient LoRA expert. During training, exposure-sample next-token prediction (NTP) updates the shared base, target-filtered NTP updates the event-based experts, and Kullback-Leibler (KL)-regularized policy optimization updates the Watch-time expert; gradient routing isolates these updates, and the General Decoder supplies a stop-gradient reference. At inference, explicit route quotas allocate the fixed budget and Multi-Decoder Constrained Beam Search reduces cross-route overlap. We publicly release Kwai26, a large-scale multi-objective benchmark with 1.31 billion raw item-level records, 31.85 million Item-ID entries, and 25.03 million items with valid Semantic IDs, together with predefined splits and an evaluation protocol. Under the same 512-item retrieval budget, Multi-Decoder OneRec improves over the single-decoder OneRec baseline by 1.69%-5.62% across four Recall@512 metrics. In a production A/B test, it yields relative gains of 0.37% in app usage time per device, 0.19% in Day-7 retained users, 0.19% in devices with at least one share, and 2.09% in new-content Cold-Start. These results show that generative retrieval can combine shared modeling with objective-specific control and complementary candidate generation.

</details>


### [36] [ASARL: Autonomous Social-Aware Relevance Learning for QQ Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26593)
*Tao Su,Jinjing Hu,Xiao Wang,Xingzhong Cao,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ASARL框架，通过多智能体数据策展和三阶段训练（社会上下文训练、偏好引导优化、社会蒸馏），解决社交搜索中非正式语言、数据稀缺和行为动态等挑战，在QQ搜索平台显著提升了相关性和用户参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在社交搜索场景中面临语境差异、数据稀缺和行为驱动动态等约束，难以有效处理非正式、社区特有的查询表达。

Method: 提出ASARL框架，包含多智能体数据策展（ReasonAgent生成可解释标签、CriticAgent验证一致性、GenAgent增强长尾数据）和三阶段训练（社会上下文训练SCT、偏好引导优化PGO、社会蒸馏SD）。

Result: 在QQ搜索平台的离线和在线实验中，离线相关性指标和在线用户参与度指标均显著提升，标注效率也得到增强。

Conclusion: 结合自主、社会感知的数据治理与偏好对齐训练，能有效提升实际搜索系统中的相关性表现。

Abstract: The rapid growth of online social platforms has transformed communication and information retrieval, giving rise to social search, where queries-titles are typically expressed in informal, community-specific language. While large language models provide strong general-purpose semantic understanding, their effectiveness in social search is constrained by contextual discrepancy, data scarcity, and behavior-driven dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose the Autonomous Social-Aware Relevance Learning (ASARL), a fully automated framework that integrates multi-agent data curation with staged model training. ASARL leverages a collaborative agent system: ReasonAgent generates interpretable relevance labels grounded in social attributes, CriticAgent validates and ensures logical consistency, and GenAgent augments long-tail data through synthetic query-title pairs. Building on the curated dataset, ASARL employs three-stage training: Social Context Training (SCT) to capture social language patterns, Preference-Guided Optimization (PGO) to align model predictions with behavioral signals, and Social Distillation (SD) to transfer these improvements into compact models for efficient deployment. Extensive offline and online experiments on the QQ search platform demonstrate significant improvements in both offline relevance metrics and online user engagement indicators, along with enhanced annotation efficiency. These results validate the effectiveness of combining autonomous, socially grounded data governance with preference-aligned training in practical search systems.

</details>


### [37] [WhisperRec: Latent Reasoning for Efficient Foundation Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26621)
*Hao Jiang,Peiru Du,Pengfei Yao,Mengting Li,Siyuan Lou,Kuo Cai,Sheng Yu,Qiang Luo,Jian Liang,Ruiming Tang,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: WhisperRec提出了一种高效的潜在推理框架，通过将教师CoT压缩为可学习的潜在推理令牌，避免生成冗长推理过程，在保持推理质量的同时将推理吞吐量提升10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于显式思维链(CoT)的推荐方法存在两大问题：1) 生成冗长推理过程带来巨大推理开销；2) 固定CoT模板难以建模多样、动态且上下文相关的用户兴趣。

Method: 1) 提出Multi-View Adaptive CoT (MV-ACoT)从多视角构建高质量监督信号，并根据实例复杂度自适应调整推理深度；2) 设计三阶段Latent Reasoning Alignment过程，将教师CoT逐步内化为潜在表示；3) 采用课程式后训练激活潜在令牌推理。

Result: 在工业级快手数据集和公开KuaiShou LLM-Rec基准上，WhisperRec持续优于显式CoT方法和传统基线。相比显式CoT的Think和No-Think变体，SID@64分别提升17.44%和9.33%，在线推理吞吐量提升10倍以上。

Conclusion: 潜在推理范式在推荐系统中是可行且高效的，能够在保留决策相关推理信息的同时，避免自回归推理的延迟瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning capabilities, motivating their adoption as backbones for foundation recommendation models (FRMs). Existing approaches typically enhance recommendation with explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) under the Think-then-Answer paradigm. However, generating lengthy rationales introduces substantial inference overhead, while fixed CoT templates struggle to model diverse, dynamic, and context-dependent user interests. We propose WhisperRec, an efficient latent reasoning framework for FRMs. WhisperRec compresses teacher-generated CoT into learnable latent reasoning tokens, enabling a Latent-Reason-then-Answer paradigm that performs reasoning in latent space without producing verbose rationales. This design retains decision-relevant reasoning information while avoiding the latency bottleneck of autoregressive rationale generation. Specifically, it first introduces Multi-View Adaptive CoT (MV-ACoT) to construct diverse, high-quality supervision from complementary perspectives on user interests. MV-ACoT also adapts reasoning complexity to each instance, applying lightweight analysis to clear cases and targeted multi-factor reasoning to challenging ones. Building on a pre-trained FRM, WhisperRec then employs a three-stage Latent Reasoning Alignment procedure to progressively internalize teacher CoT into latent representations. Finally, curriculum-based post-training activates latent-token reasoning for downstream recommendation while preserving standard recommendation capability. Experiments on an industrial-scale Kuaishou dataset and the public Kuaishou LLM-Rec benchmark show that WhisperRec consistently outperforms explicit-CoT methods and conventional baselines. Compared with explicit CoT Think and No-Think variants, WhisperRec improves SID@64 by 17.44% and 9.33%, respectively, and achieves over 10x higher online inference throughput.

</details>


### [38] [CaIRec: Calibrated Modality Imputation for Incomplete Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26720)
*Ruiyu Liu,Xiaohao Liu,Miaomiao Cai,Yunshan Ma,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对多模态推荐系统中因模态缺失导致的跨模态结构失真和偏好适应缺口问题，提出CaIRec两阶段框架：结构插补校准（SIC）通过共享信息估计缺失模态表示并校准跨模态组织，偏好导向表示校准（PRC）在表示和关系层面进行推荐适应，通过伪缺失实例对齐和补全感知图构建提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐系统中，物品常存在模态缺失（如图片、文本缺失），现有插补方法仅优化恢复的表示本身，未考虑其与同一物品其他模态间的跨模态关系一致性，导致跨模态结构失真；同时恢复的表示缺乏排序导向监督，且模态缺失破坏了偏好传播所需的物品邻域结构，导致偏好适应缺口。

Method: 提出CaIRec两阶段框架：阶段一为结构插补校准（SIC），从可用模态推断共享信息估计缺失表征，并通过结构正则化和对应监督校准跨模态组织；阶段二为偏好导向表示校准（PRC），在表示层面构造伪缺失实例对齐恢复表示与观测表示，在关系层面构建补全感知物品图整合内容关系与协同证据。

Result: 在三个数据集上不同模态缺失设置下的实验证明了CaIRec的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CaIRec通过两阶段校准框架有效解决了模态缺失场景下的跨模态结构失真和偏好适应缺口问题，显著提升了多模态推荐在不完整数据下的性能。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal recommender systems often face incomplete modality observations, where items lack images, text, or other content features. Such incompleteness weakens item representations and degrades recommendation performance. Existing modality imputation methods estimate missing representations from available item content, but two challenges remain. First, they optimize the recovered representation itself without explicitly considering its relations with other modalities of the same item. The completed modalities may therefore form inconsistent cross-modal relations, causing Cross-modal Structural Distortion. Second, even structurally coherent recovered information may remain ineffective for personalized ranking. Recovered representations receive limited ranking-oriented guidance, while modality missingness disrupts the item neighborhoods required for preference propagation, resulting in a Preference Adaptation Gap. To address these challenges, we propose Calibrated Imputation for Incomplete Multimodal Recommendation (CaIRec), a two-stage framework. Structural Imputation Calibration (SIC) estimates missing-modality representations from shared information inferred from available modalities and calibrates their cross-modal organization through structural regularization and correspondence supervision from observed modality pairs. Preference-oriented Representation Calibration (PRC) performs recommendation-specific adaptation at both the representation and relation levels. It constructs pseudo-missing instances to align recovered representations with observed counterparts shaped by ranking supervision in the recommendation space. It further builds completion-aware item graphs by integrating completed content relations with collaborative evidence. Extensive experiments on three datasets under different modality-missing settings demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of CaIRec.

</details>


### [39] [MediaWiki Code2Code Search: Neural Retrieval for the Semantic Discovery of Open-Source Software Entities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26766)
*Francesco Tosoni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 MediaWiki Code2Code Search，一个面向语义代码检索的神经检索系统。通过对 2500+ 仓库中 129 万结构实体建立索引，采用 FAISS IVF-PQ 索引压缩 96.6%，在 6 GiB 内存约束下实现 1.85 秒中位延迟，P@10 达 0.87，显著优于 BM25 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模代码库中的代码搜索面临两大挑战：用户查询与实现细节之间的词汇鸿沟（lexical gap），以及传统 IR 的低延迟与 DL 高精度之间的权衡。现有方法难以兼顾语义理解与资源受限环境下的高效检索。

Method: 采用**拆分构建架构**（split-build architecture）：将 GPU 密集型的离线索引构建与 CPU 驱动的在线服务解耦。索引 129 万结构实体（函数、类型、模板），使用 FAISS IVF-PQ 索引将大小压缩至 168.6 MB（比 float32 基线减少 96.6%），满足 6 GiB RAM 限制，中位查询延迟 1.85 秒。

Result: 在 27 个查询的基准上，P@10 为 0.87（BM25 为 0.64），严格匹配下为 0.52（BM25 为 0.34）。在名称混淆（name-obfuscated）任务中优势最为显著，词法方法在此类任务中完全失效。

Conclusion: 该系统证明通过神经检索与高效索引压缩技术，可以在资源受限的云平台（如 Wikimedia Toolforge）上实现基于计算意图的语义代码检索，且开放了 RESTful API 与 Apache 2.0 许可的代码。

Abstract: Code search in large-scale ecosystems is often hindered by the lexical gap between user queries and implementation details, alongside the trade-off between the low latency of traditional Information Retrieval (IR) and the precision of Deep Learning (DL). We present MediaWiki Code2Code Search, a neural retrieval system for semantic code-to-code discovery. By indexing 1.29 million structural entities (functions, types, and templates) across 2,500+ MediaWiki repositories, our system enables retrieval based on computational intent rather than surface tokens. We employ a split-build architecture, decoupling GPU-intensive offline indexing from a CPU-only serving layer; our FAISS IVF-PQ index occupies 168.6 MB: a 96.6\% reduction compared to a flat float32 baseline, and achieves a median query latency of 1.85 seconds on commodity hardware, satisfying the 6 GiB RAM constraint of Wikimedia Toolforge. Our evaluation across a 27-query benchmark demonstrates superior performance over the BM25 baseline, achieving a P@10 of 0.87 compared to 0.64 (0.52 versus 0.34 for strict matching). Gains are most pronounced in name-obfuscated tasks where lexical methods fail. The system is available at https://code2codesearch.toolforge.org under the Apache 2.0 licence and provides an open RESTful API.

</details>


### [40] [Beyond Action Imitation: Learning a Decision-Aware User Simulator for Online Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26893)
*Zipeng Chen,Jiaer Zheng,Xiangyang Xu,Xinyu Lin,Zhaobin Wang,Zhaohui Liu,Qianjin Xiang,Xiaoyu Zhao,Zhuozhen Yu,Guangshuo Wang,Daxing Chen,Junwei Pan,Zhangbin Zhu,Chengguo Yin,Hao Chen,Tat-Seng Chua,Haijie Gu,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DASH是一个决策感知的用户模拟器，通过联合生成思维轨迹和预测行为动作，利用跨域异构历史数据来提升推荐和广告系统离线评估的保真度和诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的用户模拟器仅从单域交互历史推断用户偏好，且仅优化预测可观测行为（如点击），导致捕获的用户偏好不完整，易产生模型捷径，限制了模拟的保真度和诊断价值。

Method: DASH包含三个阶段：1) Context Engineering，将异构跨域历史转化为决策相关上下文并进行提示优化；2) 从强LLM中蒸馏思考轨迹作为SFT数据；3) 设计基于量规的奖励模型，从形式、内容和逻辑维度评估思维轨迹，结合行为奖励进行RL训练。

Result: 在腾讯广告平台五个异构内容域的真实数据上进行的大量实验证明了DASH在有效性、效率、保真度和诊断价值方面的优越表现。

Conclusion: DASH通过联合思维生成与行为预测的方式，有效解决了传统用户模拟器的局限性，为离线评估提供了更完整、更可靠的用户模拟方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM-based user simulation have shown promise for offline evaluation of recommendation and advertising systems. However, existing simulators typically infer user preferences from single-domain interaction histories and are primarily optimized to reproduce observable actions such as clicks. Consequently, they capture only a partial view of user preferences, while action-only prediction easily induces model shortcuts and limits both the fidelity and diagnostic value of simulation. To address these challenges, we propose DASH, a decision-aware user simulator that jointly generates thinking traces and predicts behavioral actions from heterogeneous cross-domain histories. DASH first introduces a Context Engineering stage that folds heterogeneous cross-domain histories into decision-relevant context, together with prompt optimization for effective reasoning over the folded context. To train a user simulator, DASH distills thinking trajectories from strong LLMs as SFT data, and further tailors a rubric-based reward model that evaluates thinking traces along form, content, and logic for RL training. Combined with the action reward, these signals jointly improve action prediction and thinking quality. Extensive experiments on real-world Tencent advertising data spanning five heterogeneous content domains demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, fidelity, and diagnostic value of DASH.

</details>


### [41] [IMFuse: Instance-Aware Multi-Layer Fusion for LLM-Enhanced Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27002)
*Yuheng Zheng,Yu Cui,Bin Wu,Jian Zhang,Ye Feng,Can Wang,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出IMFuse，一种实例感知的多层融合策略，用于大语言模型增强的推荐系统。该方法通过自适应聚合LLM多层语义信息，并引入实例感知专家调制机制来处理不同物品的异构表示演化，在四个数据集上平均提升6.72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM增强的序列推荐方法通常只使用LLM最后一层隐藏状态，忽略了中间层包含的粗到细的互补语义信息；同时，不同物品的层表示演化存在异质性，统一层选择是次优的。

Method: IMFuse包含两个核心组件：1) 全局维度感知层偏好学习，自适应聚合多层语义信息；2) 实例感知专家调制机制，根据物品特性动态调整全局偏好，生成个性化的物品语义表示。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，IMFuse一致优于最先进基线方法，平均相对提升6.72%，且参数和计算开销增加有限。

Conclusion: 通过利用LLM中间层的互补语义信息并考虑物品级异质性，IMFuse有效提升了序列推荐的性能，证明了多层融合策略在LLM增强推荐中的价值。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly enhanced sequential recommendation by encoding rich item textual information into semantic representations. However, existing methods typically rely on the final-layer hidden states of LLMs, overlooking potentially useful semantic signals encoded in other layers. Through empirical analysis, we reveal the limitations of this practice: final-layer representations often suffer from dimensional collapse, whereas intermediate layers preserve complementary, coarse-to-fine semantic knowledge. Furthermore, we observe that different items exhibit heterogeneous layer-wise representation evolution, making a uniform layer selection sub-optimal. To bridge this gap, we propose IMFuse, an instance-aware multi-layer fusion strategy designed for LLM-enhanced recommendation. Instead of relying on a single layer, IMFuse adaptively aggregates multi-layer semantic information by learning global dimension-wise layer preferences to capture general semantic contributions. To address item-level heterogeneity, IMFuse introduces an instance-aware expert modulation mechanism that dynamically adjusts these global preferences, generating personalized, item-specific semantic representations. Extensive experiments across four real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of IMFuse. It consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines with an average relative improvement of 6.72%, while introducing limited parameter and computational overhead.

</details>


### [42] [Learning from the Future: Privileged Self-Distillation for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27055)
*Jiakai Tang,Yang Zhang,See-Kiong Ng,Xu Chen,Wen Chen,Jian Wu,Han Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出特权自蒸馏(PSD)框架，在序列推荐中利用未来交互作为训练时的特权信息，通过未来感知教师向仅前缀学生进行知识蒸馏，在不改变推理成本的情况下提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序列推荐使用因果(仅前缀)目标训练，只监督下一项目标签，忽略了非目标项目间的相对偏好。然而用户交互序列中未来交互包含了用户意图演变的信息，可以作为额外监督信号。作者希望利用这些未来交互信息进行更丰富的监督，同时保持训练与推理的一致性。

Method: 1) 在同一骨干网络上应用两种注意力掩码：未来感知掩码(教师)可访问过去和未来交互，仅前缀掩码(学生)仅访问过去交互；2) 通过知识蒸馏将教师分布传递给学生，使未来交互成为训练时监督而非推理时输入；3) 提出优势可达性门控(advantage-reachability gate)聚焦于教师中前缀能支持的信号；4) 使用动量平均教师提供稳定目标；5) 单阶段端到端优化，不改变推理模型与成本。

Result: 在多个公开基准和不同骨干网络上的一致改进，验证了PSD框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 未来交互可以作为有效的特权信息用于序列推荐训练，通过自蒸馏框架可以在不改变推理成本的情况下提升性能，教师与学生的共享骨干设计避免了额外预训练教师的需求。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders are commonly trained with one-hot next-item labels under a causal (prefix-only) objective aligned with inference. While deployment-compatible, this supervision offers little insight into relative preferences among non-target items. Yet logged interaction sequences contain an additional supervisory source: interactions following the target often reveal how user intent evolves, making the target easier to interpret. We treat these future interactions as training-only privileged information, available during learning but not at inference. This raises a natural question: can future interactions provide richer supervision while keeping training aligned with inference-time prediction?
  We propose Privileged Self-Distillation (PSD), a framework that separates learning-time information from inference-time information. PSD applies two attention masks to the same backbone: a future-aware view yields a privileged teacher distribution conditioned on past and future interactions, while a prefix-only view yields the student distribution used for deployment. Distilling the privileged distribution converts future interactions into training-only supervision rather than inference-time inputs. Since both views share a backbone, the teacher's advantage is purely informational, not architectural, removing the need for a separately pretrained teacher and letting its supervision adapt as the student evolves. PSD further uses an advantage-reachability gate to focus distillation on teacher signals likely supported by the observed prefix, along with a momentum-averaged teacher for stable targets. The framework is optimized end-to-end in a single stage, leaving the deployed model and inference cost unchanged. Experiments across public benchmarks and diverse backbones show consistent improvements.

</details>


### [43] [KAMR: Grounding Generation via Knowledge-Aligned Multi-hop Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27136)
*Xiaochen Wang,Yuan Zhong,Haoyu Wang,Ting Wang,Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出知识对齐的多跳检索器KAMR，通过区分强约束锚点三元组和弱对齐连接三元组，利用LLM构建部分对齐数据集并优化两级对比学习目标，在多跳检索和问答任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索器通常独立地对三元组进行全局语义匹配，无法有效处理多跳检索中的依赖关系；同时多跳基准数据集仅提供最终答案，缺乏查询-三元组对齐的细粒度监督，导致结构必要但语义弱对齐的事实被遗漏。

Method: 1) 提出KAMR，区分锚点三元组（受查询强约束）和连接三元组（弱对齐但结构相关）；2) 通过掩码三元组元素并提示LLM生成对应查询，构建部分对齐数据集；3) 优化两层级对比学习目标：pair-level和element-level匹配；4) 推理时先全局检索锚点，再局部扩展收集连接证据。

Result: 在四个基准、三个LLM骨干和十四个基线方法上，KAMR一致提升多跳检索和下游问答性能。

Conclusion: KAMR通过显式建模查询与三元组之间的对齐程度，结合两层级对比学习和分阶段检索策略，有效解决了多跳检索中的弱对齐问题。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation increasingly relies on multi-hop retrieval, where answering a query requires composing multiple connected knowledge-graph triplets. However, existing retrievers often rank triplets independently via global semantic matching. Moreover, many multi-hop benchmarks provide only final answers, which limits supervision for query--triplet alignment and causes structurally necessary but weakly aligned facts to be missed. To address these issues, we propose a knowledge-aligned multi-hop retriever, KAMR, which distinguishes anchor triplets that are strongly constrained by the query from connected triplets that are weakly aligned yet structurally linked to the anchors. To mitigate the lack of query-triplet alignment supervision, we build a partial alignment dataset by masking triplet elements and prompting an LLM to generate corresponding queries, and optimize two contrastive objectives for pair-level and element-level matching. At inference time, KAMR retrieves anchors globally and then expands locally to collect connected evidence. Across four benchmarks, three LLM backbones, and fourteen baselines, KAMR consistently improves multi-hop retrieval and downstream question answering performance.

</details>


### [44] [Improving Item Discoverability in e-Commerce Search via Related Intent Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27172)
*Ji Xin,Xiao Xiao,Ishan Bhatt,Vinesh Gudla,Trace Levinson,Raochuan Fan,Shishir Kumar Prasad,Prakash Putta,Tejaswi Tenneti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向电商搜索的意图条件化召回扩展系统，通过两阶段混合架构（大模型处理头部查询+蒸馏小模型处理尾部查询），将发现增强覆盖从60%扩展到80%的查询流量，且推理成本仅为教师模型的30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统搜索系统以精确匹配为主，在电商尤其是杂货领域限制了替代品、互补品及相关商品的发现能力，影响用户满意度和商业收益。

Method: 采用两阶段混合架构：1）使用闭源大语言模型（LLM）最大化头部查询的发现能力；2）通过LoRA适配器和师生蒸馏微调小语言模型（SLM），将大模型能力迁移到尾部查询。使用双重评估框架：LLM作为评判器（经人工偏好验证）和端到端会话级购买分析。

Result: 发现覆盖从约60%扩展到80%的查询流量，推理成本仅为教师模型的30%。在意图生成质量和下游检索效果上均有提升。

Conclusion: 发现增强搜索不仅提升相关性，还可作为市场平衡机制，为长尾和新兴商品提供查询条件化的曝光机会。

Abstract: Traditional search systems are optimized to retrieve items that strictly match a query, often prioritizing precision over recall. In e-commerce marketplaces and particularly grocery, this paradigm is limiting, as user satisfaction and commercial outcomes depend heavily on the discoverability of substitute, complementary, and thematically related items. In this paper, we present a scalable system for discovery-augmented search that leverages intent-conditioned recall expansion. Our approach generates implicit user intents to expand candidate recall while maintaining relevance.
  The system addresses the cost-quality tradeoff of generative retrieval through a two-stage hybrid architecture. First, we leverage closed-weight large language models (LLMs) to maximize discoverability for head queries. To extend these benefits to tail queries, we then introduce a finetuned small language model (SLM), trained via LoRA adapters and teacher-student distillation. We evaluate the system using a rigorous dual framework: (a) LLM-as-a-judge metrics validated against human preferences for semantic quality, and (b) end-to-end session-level purchase analysis. Results demonstrate that our approach improves both intent generation quality and downstream retrieval effectiveness, extending discovery coverage from approximately 60% to 80% of query traffic at roughly 30% of the teacher model's inference cost, offering a viable path for deployment in large-scale marketplaces. Beyond relevance gains, discovery-augmented search may serve as a marketplace-balancing mechanism, giving long-tail and emerging supply an opportunity for query-conditioned exposure.

</details>

### [45] [From Classification to Regression: Using a Fruitfly to Solve Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27196)
*Shady E. Ahmed,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受果蝇感知机制启发，提出一种用分类思想解决回归任务的通用框架，通过存储代表性局部模式并基于相似度加权重构来预测，适用于非线性动力系统、数据驱动回归和物理信息学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归任务依赖复杂全局代理模型，计算和存储成本高；受果蝇感知环境机制的启发，希望用有限的局部模式库替代全局模型，实现高效的非线性输入输出关系学习。

Method: 构建有限代表性局部模式库，对查询输入与存储模式进行相似度度量，然后通过加权重构组合对应的响应值进行预测。对于动力系统，采用离线-在线工作流：离线阶段从数据或控制方程中提取模式，在线阶段仅需计算相似度和聚合响应。

Result: 该方法在非线性动力系统、数据驱动回归和物理信息学习任务上取得有效结果；能降低计算和内存需求，并提供准确度、存储量和推理成本之间的显式控制。

Conclusion: 基于局部模式库的相似度加权重构方法是一种高效且可控的回归框架，特别适合科学数据中输入空间有限且重复的场景，在多个领域具有适用性。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to regression tasks using classification which is motivated by the mechanism used by fruitflies to sense their environment. Specifically, we formulate a general framework for learning nonlinear input-output relationships by replacing complex global surrogate models with a finite library of representative local patterns. Since scientific data often occupy limited and recurring regions of the input space, we generate predictions by measuring similarities between a query and stored patterns, then combining their associated responses through weighted reconstruction. We apply this approach to nonlinear dynamical systems, data-driven regression, and physics-informed learning using suitable embeddings and similarity measures. For dynamical systems, our offline-online workflow extracts patterns from data or governing equations during the offline phase, while online prediction requires only similarity evaluation and response aggregation. This structure helps us reduce computational and memory demands while providing explicit control over the trade-off among accuracy, storage, and inference cost.

</details>


### [46] [Emergent Sparsity in Frozen Random CNN Feature Extractors for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26059)
*Scott M. Norton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度强化学习智能体在使用冻结的随机初始化CNN特征提取器时，会自发产生极端稀疏的全连接层表示（FC1层仅激活1-11个神经元），且这一现象与任务复杂度、游戏内变异性相关，稀疏模式在训练早期锁定，移除这些神经元会导致性能崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现并系统性地研究深度强化学习中一个令人惊讶的现象：在不施加任何稀疏性诱导目标的情况下，使用冻结随机CNN的智能体如何在全连接层中自发形成极端稀疏的表示，并探究这一现象的本质、成因及其对任务性能的影响。

Method: 在Pong、Breakout、Space Invaders等Atari游戏中，使用冻结随机初始化的CNN特征提取器结合可训练的全连接层进行深度强化学习训练；通过神经元激活计数、参与比等指标量化稀疏性；进行宽度缩放实验、消融实验、信息瓶颈锁定时间分析以及冻结与可训练CNN的对比研究。

Result: 1) FC1层稀疏性随任务复杂度递增（Pong: 1-11个神经元; Breakout: 19-26; Space Invaders: ~42）；2) 相同游戏不同种子产生不同稀疏度，且稀疏度低的种子性能上限更低；3) 消融实验中移除活跃神经元导致四个游戏性能崩溃；4) 活跃集在训练早期（15-30M步）锁定，远早于奖励转正（35-105M步）；5) 冻结与可训练CNN在Breakout中达到相近奖励但使用不同的瓶颈结构。

Conclusion: 当输入维度远超任务内在维度时，基于冻结随机投影的梯度下降可以自动揭示问题的有效秩，无需显式的稀疏性机制。这一现象为理解深度强化学习中的表示学习提供了新视角，并暗示随机投影可能作为一种隐式正则化手段。

Abstract: We report a striking phenomenon: deep reinforcement learning agents trained with frozen, randomly initialized CNN feature extractors spontaneously develop extremely sparse fully-connected representations, without any sparsity-inducing objective. In the first fully-connected layer (FC1, $3{,}136 \to 64$), agents compress task-relevant information through as few as 1-3 neurons out of 64 for deterministic Pong (5-11 for stochastic Pong), while trainable CNNs activate 55-64 neurons under matched conditions. We establish four principal findings. First, FC1 sparsity scales with task complexity: 1-11 for Pong, 19-26 for Breakout, and $\sim$42 for Space Invaders. Width-scaling confirms this reflects task structure rather than a fixed capacity fraction. Second, within-game scaling emerges: three identical Pong seeds produce 5, 7, and 11 active neurons. The 5-neuron seed plateaus at $+14$ reward, while the others reach expert performance ($+18.4$, $+18.7$), suggesting the random projection's usable dimensionality bounds achievable performance. Third, ablation confirms necessity: removing these active neurons crashes performance across two PPO implementations and four games. Fourth, the information bottleneck commits early: a sweep shows the active set locks by 15-30M steps, while reward turns positive 35-105M steps later. A complementary finding in Breakout shows frozen and trainable CNNs reach competitive rewards via structurally different bottlenecks: frozen agents use 17-25 active neurons (participation ratio $\sim$10-14), while trainable agents use 51 (participation ratio $\sim$3.6). Finally, wherever input dimensionality dwarfs intrinsic task dimensionality, gradient descent on a frozen random projection may reveal the effective rank of the underlying problem without explicit sparsity machinery.

</details>


### [47] [Sim2Win: A Team-Agnostic, Event-Based Pre-Match Outcome Prediction and Tactical Profiling System for Football](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26061)
*Mouad Zemzoumi,Amine Abouaomar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sim2Win是一个与球队身份无关的赛前战术推荐框架，通过构建滚动战术画像和可解释战术特征比率，使用StatsBomb数据进行聚类和分类器训练，在不依赖球队身份特征的情况下预测比赛结果。LOCO评估显示平均ROC-AUC为0.704，准确率55.4%，优于ELO、Pi-Rating和GAP等基线方法，其中CatBoost取得最佳分布内性能（60.90%准确率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有职业足球赛前战术决策高度依赖主观专家分析和基于身份的球探系统，无法泛化到未见过的球队。作者希望构建一个与球队身份无关的、可泛化的赛前战术推荐框架，将比赛结果预测重构为战术决策支持问题。

Method: 使用StatsBomb开放事件数据（11项赛事，178支球队，1,411条球队-比赛记录），构建五场比赛滚动战术画像，设计四种可解释战术特征比率，通过K-Means将球队行为聚类为8种比赛风格，训练13种分类器从战术匹配表征中估计胜/平/负概率。系统不包含球队名称或身份特征，采用留一赛事交叉验证（LOCO）评估泛化能力。

Result: LOCO评估下，Sim2Win平均ROC-AUC为0.704，平均准确率55.4%，在所有21项ROC-AUC比较和19/21项准确率比较中优于ELO、Pi-Rating和GAP基线。CatBoost在分布内测试中取得最佳性能（60.90%准确率）。

Conclusion: 行为战术表征在分布偏移下能提供可迁移的预测信号，为依赖身份特征的足球预测系统提供了可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Pre-match tactical decision-making in professional football relies heavily on subjective expert analysis and identity-based scouting systems that cannot generalize to unseen teams. This paper presents Sim2Win, a team-agnostic, event-based pre-match tactical recommendation framework that reframes match outcome prediction as a tactical decision-support problem. Using StatsBomb open event data from eleven competitions spanning 178 teams and 1,411 team-match records, Sim2Win constructs five-match rolling tactical profiles, engineers four interpretable tactical feature ratios, clusters team behaviors into eight playstyles via K-Means, and trains thirteen classifiers to estimate win, draw, and loss probabilities from tactical matchup representations.
  The system operates without team names or identity features, enabling generalization to teams never seen during training. A rigorous Leave-One-Competition-Out (LOCO) evaluation demonstrates that Sim2Win achieves a mean ROC-AUC of 0.704 and mean accuracy of 55.4% on completely unseen teams, outperforming ELO, Pi-Rating, and GAP baselines on all 21 ROC-AUC comparisons and 19 of 21 accuracy comparisons. Among all evaluated models, CatBoost achieved the strongest in-distribution performance with 60.90% accuracy.
  These findings suggest that behavioral tactical representations provide transferable predictive signal under distribution shift and offer a viable alternative to identity-dependent football prediction systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: MeRLa通过元学习奖励塑形有效解决了RLHF中奖励信号稀疏的问题，在多个基准上取得显著改进，且具有理论保证和良好的可组合性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is the standard approach for aligning large language models with human preferences, but its quality is limited by static, task-agnostic reward models. This mismatch leads to sparse learning signals and suboptimal alignment. We introduce MeRLa (Meta-Learned Reward Shaping), a principled framework that meta-learns a task-aware shaping function $Φ(x,y;φ)$ across auxiliary tasks before RLHF training. The learned shaping produces a composite reward that preserves policy optimality while providing task-specific learning signals. Our meta-objective combines task discrimination, entropy regularization, and potential-based conservation for stable convergence. We provide theoretical guarantees for policy invariance, analyze representation drift sensitivity, and formally address incentive misalignment from entropy maximization. Experiments on LLaMA-3-8B across four benchmarks show consistent improvements over PPO, DPO, GRPO, and DAPO, achieving a 90.8% length-controlled win rate on AlpacaEval 2.0 and a score of 9.14 on MT-Bench, with 41% less training instability. MeRLa retains its benefits when combined with process-based and rubric-based enhanced rewards.

</details>

Motivation: 现有基于红外光谱的分子结构解析方法依赖预定义的化学式作为辅助输入，仅能进行异构体识别而非完整的无约束结构预测；Transformer模型在无约束场景下可靠性低且机制不明确，亟需改进以应对广阔的化学空间。

Result: 与基线IR-only模型相比，Top-K预测准确率提升超过10个百分点；子结构碎片分析进一步证实红外光谱编码了绝大部分相关化学信息，异构体排序模型的高性能主要源于化学空间中分子的吸收带表征不足或重叠。

Conclusion: 证明了基于实测红外光谱进行自动化无约束分子结构解析的可行性，显著拓展了AI在分析化学领域的应用范围。

Abstract: Automated molecular structure elucidation from infrared (IR) spectroscopy data has seen significant advancements in recent years, but its broad applicability is limited by a reliance on pre-determined chemical formulas provided as auxiliary model inputs. This limits model predictions to isomer identification rather than full molecular structure prediction. Although transformer models have been shown to identify molecular isomers with high accuracy, their reliability for unconstrained structure elucidation is comparatively low and poorly understood. In this work, we propose and evaluate key modifications to the traditional encoder-decoder transformer. To better address the vast chemical space of the unconstrained problem, we implement a novel Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decoder module that utilizes non-additive aggregation via linear-order statistics and the Choquet integral. We further modify the transformer to utilize these non-additive operators when aggregating spectral representations as well. Together with an auxiliary contrastive alignment loss term, these enhancements improve Top-K prediction accuracy by over 10 percentage points compared to baseline IR-only models. Through sub-structure fragment analysis of molecular predictions, we further confirm that infrared spectra encode the vast majority of relevant chemical information, implying that the higher performance of isomer-ranking models is largely due to underrepresented or overlapping absorption bands for molecules in the explored chemical space. Ultimately, by demonstrating the efficacy of automated molecular structure elucidation from measured IR spectra, this work serves to significantly broaden the utility of AI in analytical chemistry.

</details>


### [50] [Shared SFT Lessons Across Alignment, Model Organisms, and Toy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26173)
*Anton de la Fuente,Arthur Conmy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了三个不同研究领域（alignment训练、model organisms和toy models）中监督微调（SFT）的经验迁移：1) 将行为泛化经验从alignment训练迁移到toy models，发现基于行为原因的训练比仅基于示例训练能产生更好的泛化；2) 将能力保持经验从model organisms迁移到Model-Spec Midtraining alignment设置，发现混合良性on-model数据可以防止使用off-model输出训练时的能力损伤；3) 将鲁棒性经验从model organisms迁移到同一alignment设置，发现后续的良性SFT可以抹除alignment行为同时保留能力。研究表明跨领域迁移SFT经验可以提升所有相关领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于alignment训练、model organisms和toy models三个领域虽然都使用监督微调追求相同目标，但通常被分开研究。作者认为当项目共享目标时，应测试从一个领域学到的经验是否能迁移到其他领域。

Method: 方法是通过三个实验分别验证不同领域的SFT经验迁移：1) 将"Teaching Claude Why"中基于原因训练的经验从alignment训练迁移到toy models；2) 将off-model输出损害能力及on-model数据缓解的经验从model organisms迁移到alignment设置；3) 将后续SFT可能消除alignment行为的鲁棒性经验从model organisms迁移到alignment设置。

Result: 结果：1) 基于行为原因的训练比仅基于示例的训练产生更好的行为泛化；2) 混合良性on-model数据可以防止使用off-model输出训练时的大部分能力损伤，同时仍能嵌入目标行为；3) 后续的良性SFT可以抹除alignment行为同时保留能力，表明能力保持本身不能保证对后续训练的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 跨领域迁移SFT经验可以同时提升所有相关领域，建议更多研究者借鉴自身领域之外的技术。

Abstract: Alignment training, model organisms, and toy models are usually treated as separate research areas. But projects in all three frequently use supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to pursue the same underlying goals. When projects share a goal, we should test whether lessons learned from one area transfer to the other areas. We study three such transfers, each taking a lesson developed in one SFT setting and testing it in another. First, we port a lesson about behavior generalization from alignment training into toy models. Training on the reason for a behavior, as in Teaching Claude Why, can make the behavior generalize better than training on examples of the behavior alone. Second, we port a lesson about capability preservation from model organisms into the Model-Spec Midtraining alignment setting. SFT on outputs written by a model other than the student (off-model outputs) can damage capabilities when trained on. Mixing in benign on-model (and on-policy) data into our training can prevent most of this damage while still embedding the target behavior. Third, we port a lesson about robustness from model organisms into the same alignment setting. We find that follow-up benign SFT can erase the alignment behavior while preserving capabilities, showing that capability preservation alone does not ensure robustness to subsequent training. Our work illustrates how porting SFT lessons between different research fields can uplift them all, suggesting more researchers should borrow techniques from outside their own areas.

</details>


### [51] [Dynamic Parameterization Is Not Dynamic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26192)
*Zongfei Li,Yuan-yih Shang,Guozhong Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出冻结控制器审计（FCA）框架，通过缓存未扰动轨迹上的完整系数张量并回放冻结模型，区分了动态参数化中的三种混淆属性：系数变化、冻结模型的输入分配依赖性以及条件执行。实验表明，动态参数化并不等同于动态推理，功能性动态也不意味着计算节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究常将输入相关的控制器系数视为动态推理或计算节省的证据，但这混淆了系数变化、冻结模型的输入分配依赖性和条件执行三种不同属性。本文旨在厘清这些概念，并提出审计方法以准确评估动态模型的实际行为。

Method: 提出冻结控制器审计（FCA）方法：沿未扰动轨迹缓存完整系数张量，禁用控制器，然后通过跨输入重分配、token洗牌和独立校准集估计的静态配置文件来回放冻结模型。由于系数在干预前已缓存，性能变化反映的是分配依赖性，而非受扰隐藏状态重新计算控制器的反馈效应。

Result: 在七个76M FeatureGate Transformer和三个504M模型上，静态逐层配置文件分别保留了98.70%和99.43%的Correct-to-GlobalMean性能差距；层身份解释了87%~96%的系数方差；FeatureGate执行了每个Transformer块，实测推理比Dense慢30.8%；在MUDDPythia-1.4B上，跨输入重分配和token洗牌使NLL分别增加1.9067和2.9637。

Conclusion: 动态参数化本身并不建立动态推理，功能性动态也不意味着计算节省。关于动态模型的声明应分别报告系数变化、冻结模型的功能依赖性以及实际执行情况。

Abstract: Input-dependent controller coefficients are often treated as evidence of dynamic inference or computational savings. This interpretation conflates three properties: coefficient variation, dependence of a frozen model on how coefficients are assigned to inputs, and conditional execution. We focus on the second property and formulate a general principle of frozen-controller auditing. We provide one concrete implementation, Frozen-Controller Auditing (FCA), which caches the complete coefficient tensor along an unperturbed trajectory, disables the controller, and replays the frozen model with cross-input reassignment, token shuffling, and static profiles estimated from an independent calibration set. Because the coefficients are cached before any intervention, performance changes under replay measure assignment dependence without feedback from recomputing the controller on perturbed hidden states. Across seven independently trained 76M FeatureGate Transformers and three 504M models, static layerwise profiles retain 98.70% and 99.43% of the Correct-to-GlobalMean performance gap, respectively. Layer identity explains 87% to 96% of the coefficient variance. FeatureGate nevertheless executes every Transformer block, and its measured inference is 30.8% slower than Dense. On the public MUDDPythia-1.4B checkpoint, cross-input reassignment and token shuffling increase NLL by 1.9067 and 2.9637, respectively. These penalties show that the model depends strongly on content-conditioned cross-layer assignment. MUDDPythia also executes every Transformer block. The results show that dynamic parameterization alone does not establish dynamic inference and that functional dynamics do not establish computational savings. Claims about dynamic models should separately report coefficient variation, functional dependence of the frozen model, and actual execution.

</details>


### [52] [Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26246)
*Fangxu Yu,Zinan Lin,Xiaodong Liu,Weijia Xu,Michael Xu,Tianyi Zhou,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出弱到强在线策略蒸馏（W2S-OPD）框架，通过构造包含正负模型对比对的代理教师，利用logit差分提取能力方向，让强学生模型在自身rollout上蒸馏，在数学和代码基准上超越传统OPD方法，即使所有监督源都弱于学生也能持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线策略蒸馏（OPD）假设教师至少与学生一样强，但前沿场景中缺乏更强的教师模型；同时多专家合并需要大规模训练成本。本文旨在解决"弱无法教强"的困境，利用多个弱模型提升强学生。

Method: W2S-OPD：1）构造正负模型对比对（如RL后专家vs预RL初始化、大基座vs小基座、带正确提示vs错误提示的小基座）；2）计算logit差分提取能力方向；3）将该方向加到学生基座模型上构建代理教师，使其分布邻近学生；4）学生在其自身rollout上最小化逐token反向KL散度进行蒸馏。

Result: 在4个数学和3个代码基准上，W2S-OPD优于传统OPD；学生能超越领域教师；即使所有监督源都弱于学生仍能持续提升。不同对比对提取不同信号：RL后对比和提示对比强调推理框架，规模对比强调求解过程。

Conclusion: W2S-OPD打破了OPD需强教师的限制，证明了弱模型组合可有效指导强模型，为LLM能力迁移提供了低成本、可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD), which aligns a student with the teacher's token-level distribution on the student's own rollouts, is an effective paradigm for transferring capabilities across LLMs. Prevailing approaches assume a teacher at least as capable as the student: they either distill a larger model into a smaller one, which fails at the frontier where no larger teacher exists, or consolidate multiple domain experts trained from a shared base, which requires costly training at the student's scale. We introduce Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation (W2S-OPD), a simple yet effective OPD framework that improves the strong student by distilling from multiple weak models. W2S-OPD constructs a proxy teacher in logit space from a contrast pair of a positive and a negative model, both smaller than the student and cheap to obtain. Their logit difference isolates the capability direction, which is added to the student's own base model, yielding a proxy teacher that couples this direction while staying distributionally adjacent to the student. The student then distills it by minimizing the per-token reverse KL on its own rollouts. We instantiate the contrast pair as i) a post-RL expert against its pre-RL initialization, isolating the skill RL instills, ii) a larger against a smaller base model, isolating the capability from scale, and iii) a small base model with correct versus wrong hints, isolating the instance-level direction toward the solution. Across four math and three code benchmarks, W2S-OPD outperforms OPD, enables the student to surpass the domain teacher, and keeps improving the student even when every supervision source is weaker. Analysis shows different contrasts yield distinct signals: the post-RL and hint contrasts emphasize reasoning frameworks, while the scale contrast emphasizes the solving procedure. Our code will be available at https://github.com/Yu-Fangxu/W2S-OPD.

</details>


### [53] [Between Gradient and Natural Gradient: A Continuum of LoRA Initializations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26247)
*Dianze Liu,Farshid Ghezelbash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了统一低秩适配（ULoRA）框架，将现有多种LoRA初始化方法统一为双参数族（频谱白化指数+Adam类对角指数），发现最佳配置因任务而异且常位于族内而非端点；同时提供了免搜索的ULoRA-Auto变体，在GLUE和GSM8K上达到或超过全参数微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA初始化方法（如基于梯度投影、基于曲率白化）看似不同，但缺乏统一理解；不同任务对初始化策略需求不同，而固定策略无法通用。

Method: 提出ULoRA框架，将梯度预条件初始化形式化为双参数族：（1）频谱白化指数控制特征值缩放；（2）Adam类对角指数控制逐维度缩放；通过全学习率搜索扫描该族，并设计ULoRA-Auto根据每层谱统计自动选择指数。

Result: 在RoBERTa-base的5个GLUE任务上，调优的ULoRA匹配或超越全微调；在LLaMA-2-7B的GSM8K上与最强基线持平；ULoRA-Auto在无搜索代价下接近调优上界，在可部署方法中排名前列。

Conclusion: LoRA初始化中的曲率预条件应被视为可调维度而非固定设计决策，统一的预条件族比任何单一端点策略更有效。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) fine-tunes large pretrained models at a fraction of the cost of full fine-tuning, but its performance depends strongly on how the adapters are initialized. Recent schemes initialize the adapters from the downstream loss gradient: some project the raw gradient onto its top directions, while others first whiten it with an estimate of the loss curvature. We show that these seemingly distinct methods are points on a single continuum: a two-parameter family of preconditioned gradient initializations, which we call Unified LoRA (ULoRA), governed by a spectral whitening exponent and an Adam-like diagonal exponent. Sweeping this family under a full learning-rate search, we find that no single fixed preconditioning strength dominates: the best operating point is task-dependent and frequently lies strictly inside the family, away from the published endpoints. Treated as an upper bound of this family, a tuned ULoRA configuration matches or exceeds full fine-tuning on all five GLUE tasks with RoBERTa-base and is competitive with the strongest baselines on GSM8K with LLaMA-2-7B. Our deployable, search-free variant, ULoRA-Auto, selects per-layer exponents from measured spectral statistics, approaches this upper bound at no additional search cost, and ranks at or near the top among deployable LoRA methods. Our results show that a principled design space for LoRA initialization and curvature preconditioning should be treated as a tunable dimension rather than a fixed design decision.

</details>


### [54] [Early Verdicts, Better Budgets: Sequential Adaptive Rollout Allocation for Compute-Efficient RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26253)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SARA（序列自适应 rollout 分配），通过最优停止理论在强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）框架下，在rollout生成过程中动态识别并放弃饱和提示组，将释放的预算重新分配给新提示，从而在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅减少rollout开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中大量采样提示产生饱和组（全部正确或全部错误），其零奖励方差导致无策略梯度信号。现有方案要么超额采样后丢弃饱和组（动态采样，代价高昂），要么在采样前预测难度（策略漂移时脆弱）。这些方法浪费了本可避免的rollout。

Method: SARA将每步rollout收集建模为预算约束下的序列分配（最优停止）问题。维护每个提示成功率的Beta后验分布，利用封闭形式的组有效性预测器，应用双阈值SPRT风格决策规则：早期探测若干rollout后，对有效组承诺、对饱和组放弃，释放的预算重新分配给新提示。无需额外预测rollout。

Result: 在数学推理和规划任务上，使用1.5B/3B模型在单GPU上，SARA与DPS（均低于DS oracle）性能匹配，但比DS少用22%的rollout；SARA与DPS组合获得最佳准确率（略高于DS），且比DS少用67%的rollout（近乎均匀成本）。

Conclusion: SARA有效解决了RLVR中饱和组浪费rollout的问题，提供了理论上的放弃可靠性、期望rollout节省、固定预算收益优势，以及与GRPO梯度范数的有效组收益联系。该方法无需额外预测rollout，可与现有动态采样方法互补。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is bottlenecked by rollout generation, yet many sampled prompts produce saturated groups (all responses correct or all incorrect) whose zero reward variance yields no policy-gradient signal. Existing remedies either oversample a larger candidate pool and discard saturated prompts (dynamic sampling), paying heavy extra rollouts, or predict prompt difficulty before sampling, which is fragile under a shifting policy. We observe that a group's effectiveness is usually decided early, within the first few of its rollouts, so spending a full group on an already-decided prompt is wasteful. We cast per-step rollout collection as a budget-constrained sequential allocation (optimal stopping) problem and introduce SARA (Sequential Adaptive Rollout Allocation). SARA maintains a Beta posterior over each prompt's success rate, evaluates a closed-form predictor of group effectiveness, and applies a two-threshold, SPRT-style rule that commits effective groups, abandons saturated ones after a short probe, and reallocates the freed budget to fresh prompts, without any extra prediction rollouts. We prove abandonment reliability, expected rollout savings, fixed-budget yield dominance, and a link between effective-group yield and the GRPO gradient norm. On mathematical reasoning and planning with 1.5B/3B models on a single GPU, SARA matches DPS (both below the DS oracle) while using 22% fewer rollouts than DS; composing SARA with DPS yields the best accuracy, slightly above DS, at 67% fewer rollouts (near-uniform cost).

</details>


### [55] [Top-$k$ Pareto Bandits: Hypervolume Regret for Multi-Objective Slate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26273)
*Nicolas Gutowski,Fabien Chhel,Alexandre Letard,Sylvain Lamprier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机多目标臂问题，提出THV-UCB算法，通过贪婪选择k个臂来近似Pareto前沿，并建立了无间隙遗憾界$\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{nkT})$和间隙依赖界$\tilde{O}(nk^{2.5}/Δ_{\min})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多目标应用中，传统方法旨在识别单个最优臂，但实际中通常需要维护一个能够近似Pareto前沿的小规模动作集合。本文形式化了通过支配超体积来衡量所选臂子集质量的遗憾目标。

Method: 提出THV-UCB算法，基于臂对超体积的边际贡献的乐观估计进行贪婪选择。利用单调子模函数贪婪最大化的近似保证($α=1-1/e$)，定义α-近似超体积遗憾。

Result: 建立了无间隙遗憾界$\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{nkT})$和间隙依赖界$\tilde{O}(nk^{2.5}/Δ_{\min})$，后者在臂充分分离时关于T为多对数级别。

Conclusion: 理论结果支持使用小子集来近似多目标应用中的Pareto前沿。

Abstract: We consider a stochastic multi-objective bandit problem where, at each round, the agent selects a slate of $k$ arms and observes their $d$-dimensional reward vectors under semi-bandit feedback. We do not aim at identifying a single optimal arm; instead, we consider the problem of maintaining a small set of actions that jointly approximate the Pareto frontier. We formalize this objective through the dominated hypervolume induced by the selected subset of arms, and define an $α$-approximate hypervolume regret with respect to the best size-$k$ subset achievable in hindsight, where $α= 1 - 1/e$ reflects the approximation guarantee of greedy maximization for monotone submodular functions. To address this problem, we introduce \textit{THV-UCB}, an optimistic algorithm that selects arms greedily based on optimistic estimates of their marginal hypervolume contributions. We establish a gap-free regret bound $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{nkT})$ that holds on every instance, together with a gap-dependent bound $\tilde{O}(nk^{2.5}/Δ_{\min})$ that becomes polylogarithmic in $T$ once the arms are sufficiently well separated. Our results provide theoretical support for using small subsets to approximate Pareto fronts in various multi-objective applications.

</details>


### [56] [FloDR: An invertible dimensionality reduction method based on a normalising flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26278)
*Abdallah Baraka,Daniel Probst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FloDR是一种基于可逆归一化流的降维方法，通过保留所有输出坐标（而非仅前两维）实现精确逆映射和密度估计，并利用条件扩散和隐藏对比度两种诊断可视化来评估嵌入质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-SNE和UMAP等常见降维方法丢弃了高维数据中的大量信息，导致二维嵌入中的距离、聚类间关系、空白区域意义以及隐藏结构等信息不可见，用户容易过度解读嵌入结果。

Method: FloDR使用可逆归一化流进行降维，仅用前两个输出坐标生成二维嵌入，但保留其余坐标不被丢弃。由于映射是可逆的，可以计算精确逆映射和精确密度，从而生成两种诊断可视化：条件扩散（衡量每个嵌入位置在输入单位下原始数据未确定的程度）和隐藏对比度（衡量两个绘制坐标丢弃了多少关于标签对比的信息）。两种可视化均通过预设检验与保留数据及bootstrap置信度进行评估。

Result: FloDR能够提供精确的逆映射和密度估计，并基于此生成条件扩散和隐藏对比度两种诊断可视化，这些可视化通过统计检验和bootstrap置信度进行验证，未通过检验的场会被标记为"拒绝"。

Conclusion: FloDR通过可逆归一化流保留了完整的信息流，使得二维嵌入的可解释性和可信度得到提升，用户能够通过诊断可视化了解嵌入中哪些信息被保留、哪些被丢弃。

Abstract: It is common for two-dimensional embeddings of high-dimensional data to be read far beyond what they can support. Distances in and between clusters, the meaning behind empty spaces, and the amount of structure hidden at each point are generally invisible in the output of methods such as t-SNE and UMAP. This is because the information that could support the meaning of these properties is discarded during the optimisation process. Here, we present FloDR, a dimensionality reduction method that embeds data through an invertible normalising flow. While FloDR only uses the first two output coordinates to create a two-dimensional embedding, it retains the remaining coordinates rather than discarding them. In addition to the embedding, an exact inverse and an exact density are properties of a trained mapping, which enable diagnostic visualisations that are computed from the exact inverse of the model that drew the layout rather than from an approximate one. Specifically, we draw two fields, the conditional spread, which measures how much of the original data remains undetermined at each embedding position in input units, and the hidden contrast, which measures how much information about a labelled contrast the two plotted coordinates discard. Both fields are rendered with a prespecified test against a held out portion of the input data and a bootstrap confidence. A field that fails the test is reported as refused.

</details>


### [57] [Entity Resolution in Practice: Lessons from a Self-Serve Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26298)
*Kaushik Pavani,Ganga Aluri,Pravin Jadhav,Neeraj Prasad,Kiran Sanka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过构建和评估一个自助式实体解析系统（涵盖864到500万条记录的六个基准），总结了三条现有文献未提及的经验教训：（1）没有通用的最优匹配算法，建议在每个数据集上训练多种算法并通过自动竞赛选择最佳；（2）精确率和召回率需要分别用硬规则否决和多样化候选检索来修复，而非共享阈值；（3）一次假阳性匹配可能通过传递性导致整个实体组被错误合并，因此必须主动重新验证跨组合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体解析文献缺乏对自助式（self-serve）系统实际部署中遇到的系统性问题的总结，尤其是当系统无法预知下一个数据集时，如何设计通用且鲁棒的匹配流水线。

Method: 在六个基准数据集（864至500万条记录）上构建和评估自助式实体解析系统，通过对比多种算法、分析精确率与召回率的分离修复策略、以及研究传递性合并带来的连锁错误，总结出三条经验教训。

Result: （1）单一算法无法在所有数据集上最优，需采用多算法训练加自动竞赛（bake-off）策略；（2）精确率依赖硬规则否决，召回率依赖多样化候选检索，不能共享阈值；（3）传递性假设（A≈B且B≈C⇒A≈C）会导致单个错误链接连锁传播，必须对跨组合并进行主动重新验证。

Conclusion: 自助式实体解析系统需要多算法并行训练、分离优化精确率与召回率、以及打破传递性假设以主动验证跨组合并，这些经验可帮助从业者避免大量试错实验。

Abstract: We built and evaluated a self-serve entity resolution (ER) system on six benchmarks spanning 864 to 5M records, and three lessons emerged that are absent from existing ER literature.
